cat > rust_simulator/src/products.rs << 'EOF'
//! Round-agnostic product catalogue.
//!
//! Every product the simulator knows about lives here.
//! To add a new product:
//!   1. Add a variant to `Product`.
//!   2. Add its position limit to `Product::position_limit`.
//!   3. Add its round membership to `Product::for_round`.
//!   4. Fill in `generate_fair_value_path` for that product (or leave the`
//!      USER_MUST_IMPLEMENT panic and fill it later).
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::fmt;
/// Every product the simulator can trade.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE")]
pub enum Product {`
    // ── Tutorial / Round 0 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────`
    Emeralds,`
    Tomatoes,`
    // ── Round 1 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
    /// Fixed-fair-value pure market-making product (like EMERALDS).`
    /// Position limit: 80.`
    AshCoatedOsmium,`
    /// Directional / mean-reverting / latent-process product (like TOMATOES).`
    /// Position limit: 80.`
    IntarianPepperRoot,`
}
impl fmt::Display for Product {`
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {`
        write!(f, "{}", self.name())`
    }`
}`
`
impl Product {`
    /// Official product name string used in logs / JSON.`
    pub fn name(self) -> &'static str {`
        match self {`
            Product::Emeralds => "EMERALDS",`
            Product::Tomatoes => "TOMATOES",`
            Product::AshCoatedOsmium => "ASH_COATED_OSMIUM",`
            Product::IntarianPepperRoot => "INTARIAN_PEPPER_ROOT",`
        }`
    }`
`
    /// Maximum absolute position allowed by IMC rules for this product.`
    pub fn position_limit(self) -> i32 {`
        match self {`
            Product::Emeralds => 80,`
            Product::Tomatoes => 80,`
            Product::AshCoatedOsmium => 80,`
            Product::IntarianPepperRoot => 80,`
        }`
    }`
`
    /// Which products are active in a given round.`
    /// Round 0 = tutorial.  Round 1 = first real round.`
    pub fn for_round(round: u32) -> Vec<Product> {`
        match round {`
            0 => vec![Product::Emeralds, Product::Tomatoes],`
            1 => vec![Product::AshCoatedOsmium, Product::IntarianPepperRoot],`
            _ => panic!("Unknown round {round}; add its products to Product::for_round"),`
        }`
    }`
`
    /// Is this product a "fixed fair-value" market-making product?`
    /// (As opposed to a latent-process / directional product.)`
    pub fn is_fixed_fair_value(self) -> bool {`
        matches!(self, Product::Emeralds | Product::AshCoatedOsmium)`
    }`
}
EOF
cat > rust_simulator/src/price_signals.rs << 'EOF'
//! ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//! PLUGGABLE PRICE-SIGNAL HOOK
//! ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//!
//! This is the ONE place you need to edit to implement your own fair-value
//! process for each product.
//!
//! CONTRACT
//! ────────
//! `generate_fair_value_path(product, seed, params, n_steps)` must return a
//! `Vec<f64>` of length `n_steps` where each element is the *latent* fair value
//! of the product at that simulation tick.
//!
//! The downstream simulator samples integer quotes symmetrically around this
//! fair value, so realistic continuous values are fine — the book-generation
//! layer rounds to integers.
//!
//! PLACEHOLDER BEHAVIOUR
//! ─────────────────────
//! Products whose process has not yet been implemented will `panic!` with a
//! clear error message.  Replace the `todo!` arms as you build out your models.
//!
//! ADDING A NEW PROCESS
//! ────────────────────
//! 1. Add a variant to `PriceSignalParams` if the process needs bespoke params.
//! 2. Add a branch inside `generate_fair_value_path`.
//! 3. Implement the generator function.
//!
//! NO OTHER FILE NEEDS TO CHANGE.
use rand::prelude::*;
use rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng;
use crate::products::Product;
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Parameter structs
// One per product family; keep them small and serialisable.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/// Parameters for the EMERALDS / ASH_COATED_OSMIUM fixed-fair-value family.
/// The fair value is a constant; there is nothing to calibrate for the process
/// itself (all the calibration lives in the book-generation parameters).
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct FixedFairValueParams {`
    /// The single fixed fair value (e.g. 10_000 for EMERALDS).`
    pub fair_value: f64,`
}
/// Parameters for the TOMATOES / INTARIAN_PEPPER_ROOT latent-process family.
///
/// Default model: Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (mean-reverting diffusion).
///   x_{t+1} = x_t + kappa*(mu - x_t)*dt + sigma*sqrt(dt)*Z,  Z ~ N(0,1)
///
/// For a pure random-walk (zero mean-reversion) set `kappa = 0.0`.
/// USER: replace or extend this with whatever process you calibrate from data.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct LatentProcessParams {`
    /// Long-run mean.`
    pub mu: f64,`
    /// Mean-reversion speed (≥ 0).  0 = random walk.`
    pub kappa: f64,`
    /// Diffusion coefficient (per-tick volatility when kappa=0, dt=1).`
    pub sigma: f64,`
    /// Time-step size (usually 1 / 10_000 if you think of a day as 10k ticks).`
    pub dt: f64,`
    /// Initial value of the process (sampled around mu at session start).`
    pub x0: f64,`
}
/// Union of all per-product parameter sets.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum PriceSignalParams {`
    Fixed(FixedFairValueParams),`
    LatentOU(LatentProcessParams),`
    /// Placeholder — causes a clear panic if accidentally invoked.`
    UserMustImplement { product_name: &'static str },`
}`
`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
// Default parameters`
// These are calibrated from the tutorial-round CSVs.`
// Round 1 defaults are PLACEHOLDERS — replace them with your own calibration.`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
`
impl PriceSignalParams {`
    /// Return the *default* parameters for a product.`
    /// These are used when the caller does not supply explicit params.`
    pub fn default_for(product: Product) -> Self {`
        match product {`
            // ── Tutorial / Round 0 ──────────────────────────────────────────`
            Product::Emeralds => PriceSignalParams::Fixed(FixedFairValueParams {`
                fair_value: 10_000.0,`
            }),`
            Product::Tomatoes => PriceSignalParams::LatentOU(LatentProcessParams {`
                mu: 4_000.0,`
                kappa: 0.0,   // tutorial TOMATOES is close to a random walk`
                sigma: 1.8,   // calibrated from tutorial CSV`
                dt: 1.0,`
                x0: 4_000.0,`
            }),`
`
            // ── Round 1 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
            // USER: replace these placeholders with values from your calibration`
            // scripts in /calibration/round1/.`
            Product::AshCoatedOsmium => PriceSignalParams::Fixed(FixedFairValueParams {`
                // TODO: replace 10_000.0 with the actual Round-1 fixed fair value`
                //       once you have calibrated it from the Round-1 data.`
                fair_value: 10_000.0,`
            }),`
            Product::IntarianPepperRoot => PriceSignalParams::UserMustImplement {`
                product_name: "INTARIAN_PEPPER_ROOT",`
            },`
        }`
    }`
}`
`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
// Core hook`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
`
/// Generate a fair-value path of length `n_steps` for `product`.`
///`
/// # Arguments`
/// * `product`  – which product to simulate`
/// * `seed`     – RNG seed (u64); sessions use different seeds for independence`
/// * `params`   – process parameters (use `PriceSignalParams::default_for` if`
///                you have not yet calibrated)`
/// * `n_steps`  – number of simulation ticks (e.g. 10_000)`
///`
/// # Returns`
/// A `Vec<f64>` of length exactly `n_steps`.`
///`
/// # Panics`
/// Panics with a descriptive message if the product's process has not been`
/// implemented yet.`
pub fn generate_fair_value_path(`
    product: Product,`
    seed: u64,`
    params: &PriceSignalParams,`
    n_steps: usize,`
) -> Vec<f64> {`
    match params {`
        PriceSignalParams::Fixed(p) => fixed_fair_value_path(p, n_steps),`
        PriceSignalParams::LatentOU(p) => ou_path(p, seed, n_steps),`
        PriceSignalParams::UserMustImplement { product_name } => {`
            panic!(`
                "\n\`
                ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n\`
                ║  USER MUST IMPLEMENT PRICE SIGNALS HERE                      ║\n\`
                ║                                                              ║\n\`
                ║  Product : {:<48}║\n\`
                ║                                                              ║\n\`
                ║  Steps:                                                      ║\n\`
                ║  1. Open rust_simulator/src/price_signals.rs                 ║\n\`
                ║  2. Add a PriceSignalParams variant for this product         ║\n\`
                ║  3. Implement the generator in the match arm below           ║\n\`
                ║  4. Update PriceSignalParams::default_for() to use it       ║\n\`
                ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝",`
                format!("{}  ", product_name)`
            );
        }
    }
}
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Generator implementations
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/// Fixed fair value: every tick returns the same constant.
fn fixed_fair_value_path(params: &FixedFairValueParams, n_steps: usize) -> Vec<f64> {`
    vec![params.fair_value; n_steps]`
}
/// Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process (mean-reverting diffusion).
///
/// Discretisation (Euler-Maruyama):
///   x_{t+1} = x_t + kappa*(mu - x_t)*dt + sigma*sqrt(dt)*Z
///
/// When kappa=0 this reduces to a Gaussian random walk.
fn ou_path(params: &LatentProcessParams, seed: u64, n_steps: usize) -> Vec<f64> {`
    let mut rng = ChaCha8Rng::seed_from_u64(seed);`
    let mut path = Vec::with_capacity(n_steps);`
    let mut x = params.x0;`
    let sqrt_dt = params.dt.sqrt();`
`
    for _ in 0..n_steps {`
        path.push(x);`
        let z: f64 = rng.sample(rand_distr::StandardNormal);`
        let drift = params.kappa * (params.mu - x) * params.dt;`
        let diffusion = params.sigma * sqrt_dt * z;`
        x += drift + diffusion;`
    }`
    path`
}
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Unit tests
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {`
    use super::*;`
`
    #[test]`
    fn fixed_path_has_correct_length_and_value() {`
        let p = PriceSignalParams::Fixed(FixedFairValueParams { fair_value: 10_000.0 });`
        let path = generate_fair_value_path(Product::Emeralds, 42, &p, 100);`
        assert_eq!(path.len(), 100);`
        assert!(path.iter().all(|&v| (v - 10_000.0).abs() < 1e-9));
    }
    #[test]
    fn ou_path_has_correct_length() {`
        let p = PriceSignalParams::LatentOU(LatentProcessParams {`
            mu: 4_000.0,`
            kappa: 0.01,`
            sigma: 2.0,`
            dt: 1.0,`
            x0: 4_000.0,`
        });`
        let path = generate_fair_value_path(Product::Tomatoes, 99, &p, 10_000);`
        assert_eq!(path.len(), 10_000);`
    }
    #[test]
    fn different_seeds_give_different_paths() {`
        let p = PriceSignalParams::LatentOU(LatentProcessParams {`
            mu: 4_000.0, kappa: 0.0, sigma: 2.0, dt: 1.0, x0: 4_000.0,`
        });`
        let a = generate_fair_value_path(Product::Tomatoes, 1, &p, 1_000);`
        let b = generate_fair_value_path(Product::Tomatoes, 2, &p, 1_000);`
        assert_ne!(a, b);`
    }
}

cat > rust_simulator/src/simulator.rs << 'EOF'
//! Core Monte Carlo session simulator.
//!
//! One `Session` simulates one full trading day (10_000 ticks by default) for
//! every product active in a given round.  Sessions are independent and can be
//! run in parallel with Rayon.
//!
//! Architecture
//! ────────────
//! tick loop
//!   └─ for each product
//!         1. get fair value from price-signal path
//!         2. generate bot order book around fair value
//!         3. call Python Trader.run(state)  [via FFI / subprocess]
//!         4. match trader orders against book
//!         5. simulate bot takers
//!         6. record state for policy optimiser
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use crate::price_signals::{generate_fair_value_path, PriceSignalParams};
use crate::products::Product;
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Book-generation parameters (calibrated per-product)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/// Market-structure parameters that control how bot quotes are placed.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BookParams {`
    /// Outer symmetric half-spread around fair value.`
    pub outer_spread: f64,`
    /// Inner symmetric half-spread around fair value.`
    pub inner_spread: f64,`
    /// Size of outer wall orders.`
    pub outer_size: i32,`
    /// Size of inner wall orders.`
    pub inner_size: i32,`
    /// Probability that Bot-3 (one-sided inside quote) appears at any tick.`
    pub bot3_prob: f64,`
    /// Typical size of Bot-3 quote when it appears.`
    pub bot3_size: i32,`
    /// Expected number of bot-taker events per tick (Poisson lambda).`
    pub taker_lambda: f64,`
    /// Mean taker size.`
    pub taker_size_mean: f64,`
}
impl BookParams {`
    pub fn default_for(product: Product) -> Self {`
        match product {`
            Product::Emeralds => Self {`
                outer_spread: 10.0,`
                inner_spread: 8.0,`
                outer_size: 10,`
                inner_size: 5,`
                bot3_prob: 0.3,`
                bot3_size: 3,`
                taker_lambda: 0.8,`
                taker_size_mean: 4.0,`
            },`
            Product::Tomatoes => Self {`
                outer_spread: 8.0,`
                inner_spread: 6.5,`
                outer_size: 8,`
                inner_size: 4,`
                bot3_prob: 0.4,`
                bot3_size: 2,`
                taker_lambda: 0.6,`
                taker_size_mean: 3.0,`
            },`
            // ── Round 1 placeholders ─────────────────────────────────────────`
            // USER: replace with calibrated values once you have Round-1 data.`
            Product::AshCoatedOsmium => Self {`
                outer_spread: 10.0,  // TODO: calibrate from Round-1 data`
                inner_spread: 8.0,   // TODO`
                outer_size: 10,      // TODO`
                inner_size: 5,       // TODO`
                bot3_prob: 0.3,      // TODO`
                bot3_size: 3,        // TODO`
                taker_lambda: 0.8,   // TODO`
                taker_size_mean: 4.0,// TODO`
            },`
            Product::IntarianPepperRoot => Self {`
                outer_spread: 8.0,   // TODO: calibrate from Round-1 data`
                inner_spread: 6.5,   // TODO`
                outer_size: 8,       // TODO`
                inner_size: 4,       // TODO`
                bot3_prob: 0.4,      // TODO`
                bot3_size: 2,        // TODO`
                taker_lambda: 0.6,   // TODO`
                taker_size_mean: 3.0,// TODO`
            },`
        }`
    }`
}
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Per-tick state snapshot (written to disk for the policy optimiser)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/// Rich state vector recorded at every tick for every product.
/// The policy optimiser in Python reads these to fit the sizing function.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct TickSnapshot {`
    pub session_id: u64,`
    pub tick: usize,`
    pub product: String,`
    /// Latent fair value at this tick.`
    pub fair_value: f64,`
    /// Best bid price visible in the book (integer).`
    pub best_bid: i32,`
    /// Best ask price visible in the book (integer).`
    pub best_ask: i32,`
    /// Mid price of the visible book.`
    pub mid_price: f64,`
    /// Signed mispricing = fair_value − mid_price.`
    pub mispricing: f64,`
    /// Number of ticks remaining in the session (10_000 − tick).`
    pub time_left: usize,`
    /// Rolling realised vol of the fair-value path over the last 100 ticks.`
    pub current_vol: f64,`
    /// Trader's inventory at start of this tick.`
    pub inventory: i32,`
    /// Half-spread of the visible book.`
    pub spread_half: f64,`
    /// ── LABEL ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
    /// Empirically optimal position size in hindsight for this tick.`
    /// Computed by the Python side after the session finishes.`
    /// Set to i32::MIN (sentinel) until filled in.`
    pub optimal_position_hindsight: i32,`
}`
`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
// Session result`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
`
/// Everything a single MC session produces.`
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]`
pub struct SessionResult {`
    pub session_id: u64,`
    pub round: u32,`
    /// Final PnL per product (cash + MTM).`
    pub pnl_by_product: HashMap<String, f64>,`
    /// Total PnL across all products.`
    pub total_pnl: f64,`
    /// Full tick-by-tick snapshots (only written when policy optimiser is on).`
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]`
    pub snapshots: Vec<TickSnapshot>,`
}`
`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
// Simulator configuration`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
`
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]`
pub struct SimConfig {`
    pub round: u32,`
    pub n_steps: usize,`
    /// If true, record full TickSnapshots for the policy optimiser.`
    pub record_snapshots: bool,`
    /// Override price-signal params per product (None → use defaults).`
    pub signal_params: HashMap<String, PriceSignalParams>,`
    /// Override book params per product (None → use defaults).`
    pub book_params: HashMap<String, BookParams>,`
}`
`
impl Default for SimConfig {`
    fn default() -> Self {`
        Self {`
            round: 0,`
            n_steps: 10_000,`
            record_snapshots: false,`
            signal_params: HashMap::new(),`
            book_params: HashMap::new(),`
        }`
    }`
}`
`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
// Order book helpers (pure, zero-alloc-hot-path where possible)`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
`
/// A single price level in the synthetic order book.`
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]`
struct Level {`
    price: i32,`
    size: i32,`
}`
`
/// Minimal synthetic order book visible to the Trader.`
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]`
pub struct SyntheticBook {`
    pub bids: Vec<Level>,   // sorted descending by price`
    pub asks: Vec<Level>,   // sorted ascending by price`
}`
`
impl SyntheticBook {`
    /// Best bid price (returns None if empty).`
    pub fn best_bid(&self) -> Option<i32> {`
        self.bids.first().map(|l| l.price)`
    }`
    /// Best ask price (returns None if empty).`
    pub fn best_ask(&self) -> Option<i32> {`
        self.asks.first().map(|l| l.price)`
    }`
    /// Midpoint of the visible book.`
    pub fn mid(&self) -> f64 {`
        let b = self.best_bid().unwrap_or(0) as f64;`
        let a = self.best_ask().unwrap_or(0) as f64;`
        (b + a) / 2.0`
    }`
    /// Half-spread.`
    pub fn half_spread(&self) -> f64 {`
        let b = self.best_bid().unwrap_or(0) as f64;`
        let a = self.best_ask().unwrap_or(0) as f64;`
        (a - b) / 2.0`
    }`
}`
`
/// Generate the synthetic bot order book for a given fair value.`
pub fn make_book(fv: f64, bp: &BookParams, rng: &mut impl rand::Rng) -> SyntheticBook {`
    let fv_int = fv.round() as i32;`
`
    // Bot-1: outer symmetric wall`
    let outer_bid = fv_int - bp.outer_spread.round() as i32;`
    let outer_ask = fv_int + bp.outer_spread.round() as i32;`
`
    // Bot-2: inner symmetric wall`
    let inner_bid = fv_int - bp.inner_spread.round() as i32;`
    let inner_ask = fv_int + bp.inner_spread.round() as i32;`
`
    let mut bids = vec![`
        Level { price: inner_bid, size: bp.inner_size },`
        Level { price: outer_bid, size: bp.outer_size },`
    ];`
    let mut asks = vec![`
        Level { price: inner_ask, size: bp.inner_size },`
        Level { price: outer_ask, size: bp.outer_size },`
    ];`
`
    // Bot-3: optional one-sided inside quote`
    if rng.gen::<f64>() < bp.bot3_prob {`
        let side_is_bid: bool = rng.gen();`
        if side_is_bid {`
            let price = inner_bid + 1;`
            bids.insert(0, Level { price, size: bp.bot3_size });`
        } else {`
            let price = inner_ask - 1;`
            asks.insert(0, Level { price, size: bp.bot3_size });`
        }`
    }`
`
    // Sort bids desc, asks asc`
    bids.sort_by(|a, b| b.price.cmp(&a.price));`
    asks.sort_by(|a, b| a.price.cmp(&b.price));`
`
    SyntheticBook { bids, asks }`
}`
`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
// Utility: rolling volatility`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
`
/// Compute the sample standard deviation of the last `window` returns in `path`.`
pub fn rolling_vol(path: &[f64], tick: usize, window: usize) -> f64 {`
    if tick < 2 {`
        return 0.0;`
    }`
    let start = tick.saturating_sub(window);`
    let returns: Vec<f64> = path[start..tick]`
        .windows(2)`
        .map(|w| w[1] - w[0])`
        .collect();`
    if returns.is_empty() {`
        return 0.0;`
    }`
    let mean = returns.iter().sum::<f64>() / returns.len() as f64;`
    let var = returns.iter().map(|r| (r - mean).powi(2)).sum::<f64>() / returns.len() as f64;`
    var.sqrt()`
}`
EOF`
`
cat > rust_simulator/src/main.rs << 'EOF'`
//! prosperity4mcbt Rust simulator — entry point.`
//!`
//! Usage (called by Python CLI, not usually invoked directly):`
//!   rust_simulator --round 0 --sessions 1000 --steps 10000 \`
//!                  --out /tmp/results.json [--snapshots]`
//!`
//! The binary writes a JSON array of SessionResult to stdout (or --out file).`
//! Python CLI reads that array to build the dashboard bundle.`
`
mod price_signals;`
mod products;`
mod simulator;`
`
use clap::Parser;`
use rayon::prelude::*;`
use serde_json;`
use std::collections::HashMap;`
use std::path::PathBuf;`
`
use price_signals::PriceSignalParams;`
use products::Product;`
use simulator::{BookParams, SessionResult, SimConfig, TickSnapshot};`
`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
// CLI`
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
`
#[derive(Parser, Debug)]`
#[command(name = "rust_simulator", about = "IMC Prosperity 4 Monte Carlo engine")]`
struct Cli {`
    /// Which round to simulate (0 = tutorial, 1 = Round 1, …).`
    #[arg(long, default_value_t = 0)]`
    round: u32,`
`
    /// Number of independent Monte Carlo sessions.`
    #[arg(long, default_value_t = 100)]`
    sessions: u64,`
`
    /// Ticks per session.`
    #[arg(long, default_value_t = 10_000)]`
    steps: usize,`
`
    /// Write full tick snapshots (needed for --optimize-policy).`
    #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]`
    snapshots: bool,`
`
    /// Output JSON file (default: stdout).`
    #[arg(long)]`
    out: Option<PathBuf>,`
`
    /// Seed offset so different Python workers don't overlap.`
    #[arg(long, default_value_t = 0)]`
    seed_offset: u64,`
}
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Main
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
fn main() {`
    let cli = Cli::parse();`
`
    let products = Product::for_round(cli.round);`
`
    let config = SimConfig {`
        round: cli.round,`
        n_steps: cli.steps,`
        record_snapshots: cli.snapshots,`
        signal_params: HashMap::new(),  // use defaults`
        book_params: HashMap::new(),    // use defaults`
    };`
`
    // ── Parallel Monte Carlo sessions ───────────────────────────────────────`
    let results: Vec<SessionResult> = (0..cli.sessions)`
        .into_par_iter()
        .map(|session_id| {`
            run_session(session_id + cli.seed_offset, &products, &config)`
        })
        .collect();
    // ── Serialise ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&results)
        .expect("Failed to serialise results");
    if let Some(out_path) = cli.out {`
        std::fs::write(&out_path, &json)`
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("Cannot write to {:?}: {}", out_path, e));
        eprintln!(`
            "[rust_simulator] Wrote {} sessions to {}",`
            results.len(),`
            out_path.display()
        );
    } else {`
        println!("{}", json);
    }
}
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Single session
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
fn run_session(seed: u64, products: &[Product], config: &SimConfig) -> SessionResult {`
    use rand::prelude::*;`
    use rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng;`
`
    let mut rng = ChaCha8Rng::seed_from_u64(seed);`
`
    // ── Generate fair-value paths for each product ───────────────────────────`
    let mut fv_paths: HashMap<Product, Vec<f64>> = HashMap::new();`
    for &product in products {`
        let params = config`
            .signal_params`
            .get(product.name())`
            .cloned()`
            .unwrap_or_else(|| PriceSignalParams::default_for(product));`
        // Give each product a different sub-seed derived from the session seed.`
        let product_seed = seed`
            .wrapping_mul(0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15)`
            .wrapping_add(product.name().len() as u64);`
        let path = price_signals::generate_fair_value_path(`
            product,`
            product_seed,`
            &params,`
            config.n_steps,`
        );`
        fv_paths.insert(product, path);`
    }`
`
    // ── Per-product state ────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
    let mut cash: HashMap<Product, f64> = products.iter().map(|&p| (p, 0.0)).collect();
    let mut inventory: HashMap<Product, i32> = products.iter().map(|&p| (p, 0)).collect();
    let mut snapshots: Vec<TickSnapshot> = Vec::new();
    // ── Tick loop ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // NOTE: This is the structural skeleton.  The actual Python Trader.run()
    // call is orchestrated by the Python CLI via subprocess/FFI.  The Rust
    // binary here produces the *market environment* (paths + book states) and
    // accounting, while the Python worker in scripts/worker.py drives the
    // Trader.  For the pure-Rust fast path (no Python trader) this loop
    // computes the accounting with a no-op trader (zero orders).
    for tick in 0..config.n_steps {`
        for &product in products {`
            let fv = fv_paths[&product][tick];`
            let bp = config`
                .book_params`
                .get(product.name())`
                .cloned()`
                .unwrap_or_else(|| BookParams::default_for(product));`
`
            let book = simulator::make_book(fv, &bp, &mut rng);`
`
            if config.record_snapshots {`
                let mid = book.mid();`
                let vol = simulator::rolling_vol(&fv_paths[&product], tick, 100);`
                snapshots.push(TickSnapshot {`
                    session_id: seed,`
                    tick,`
                    product: product.name().to_string(),`
                    fair_value: fv,`
                    best_bid: book.best_bid().unwrap_or(0),`
                    best_ask: book.best_ask().unwrap_or(0),`
                    mid_price: mid,`
                    mispricing: fv - mid,`
                    time_left: config.n_steps - tick,`
                    current_vol: vol,`
                    inventory: inventory[&product],`
                    spread_half: book.half_spread(),`
                    optimal_position_hindsight: i32::MIN, // filled in by Python`
                });`
            }`
`
            // No-op trader: do nothing (Python trader path is via worker.py).`
            // The accounting below therefore leaves cash/inventory unchanged.`
        }`
    }
    // ── Final MTM PnL ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    let mut pnl_by_product: HashMap<String, f64> = HashMap::new();
    let mut total_pnl = 0.0_f64;
    for &product in products {`
        let final_fv = fv_paths[&product][config.n_steps - 1];`
        let mtm = cash[&product] + inventory[&product] as f64 * final_fv;`
        pnl_by_product.insert(product.name().to_string(), mtm);`
        total_pnl += mtm;`
    }
    SessionResult {`
        session_id: seed,`
        round: config.round,`
        pnl_by_product,`
        total_pnl,`
        snapshots,`
    }
}
EOF

cat > backtester/src/prosperity4mcbt/round1_products.py << 'EOF'
"""`
Round 1 product definitions for the Monte Carlo backtester.`
`
Products`
--------`
ASH_COATED_OSMIUM    – fixed-fair-value market-making (like EMERALDS)`
INTARIAN_PEPPER_ROOT – latent-process directional / mean-reverting (like TOMATOES)`
`
Both have position limit 80.`
`
This module is the Python mirror of rust_simulator/src/products.rs.`
Add calibrated parameters here once you have Round-1 data.`
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Product metadata
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
ROUND1_PRODUCTS: dict[str, int] = {`
    "ASH_COATED_OSMIUM": 80,`
    "INTARIAN_PEPPER_ROOT": 80,`
}
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Pluggable price-signal hook (Python side)
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def generate_price_signals(`
    round_id: int,`
    product: str,`
    seed: int,`
    n_steps: int = 10_000,`
    **kwargs,`
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """`
    Generate a synthetic price-signal path for `product` in `round_id`.`
`
    Returns`
    -------`
    pd.DataFrame with columns:`
        tick        : int   (0 … n_steps-1)`
        fair_value  : float (latent true price)`
        mid_price   : float (integer-rounded observable mid)`
        best_bid    : int`
        best_ask    : int`
`
    This function is the PYTHON HOOK you must implement.`
`
    For Round 0 (tutorial) the implementations are in`
    backtester/src/prosperity4mcbt/tutorial_products.py.`
`
    For Round 1 fill in the two branches below once you have calibrated your`
    process from the Round-1 data CSVs.`
`
    The Rust simulator generates the same paths independently (using the same`
    seed and parameter values) — keep both in sync.`
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if round_id == 0:
        return _round0_signals(product, rng, n_steps)
    elif round_id == 1:
        return _round1_signals(product, rng, n_steps, **kwargs)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown round_id={round_id}")
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Round 0 (tutorial) — already calibrated
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _round0_signals(`
    product: str,`
    rng: np.random.Generator,`
    n_steps: int,`
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Tutorial-round price-signal generators (calibrated from tutorial CSVs)."""
    if product == "EMERALDS":
        fv = np.full(n_steps, 10_000.0)
    elif product == "TOMATOES":
        # Gaussian random walk calibrated from tutorial data.
        sigma = 1.8
        increments = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n_steps)
        fv = 4_000.0 + np.cumsum(increments) - np.cumsum(increments)[0]
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown Round-0 product: {product}")
    ticks = np.arange(n_steps)
    mid = np.round(fv).astype(int)
    return pd.DataFrame({`
        "tick": ticks,`
        "fair_value": fv,`
        "mid_price": mid.astype(float),`
        "best_bid": mid - 1,`
        "best_ask": mid + 1,`
    })
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Round 1 — USER MUST IMPLEMENT
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _round1_signals(`
    product: str,`
    rng: np.random.Generator,`
    n_steps: int,`
    **kwargs,`
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """`
    Round-1 price-signal generators.`
`
    ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗`
    ║  USER MUST IMPLEMENT PRICE SIGNALS HERE                      ║`
    ║                                                              ║`
    ║  Replace the NotImplementedError branches below with your    ║`
    ║  calibrated process once you have Round-1 data.              ║`
    ║                                                              ║`
    ║  Mirror whatever process you implement in the Rust file:     ║`
    ║  rust_simulator/src/price_signals.rs                         ║`
    ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝`
    """
    if product == "ASH_COATED_OSMIUM":
        # ── USER: replace with calibrated fixed fair value ───────────────────
        # This is the EMERALDS analogue: likely a constant or near-constant
        # fair value.  Find it by looking at where Bot-1 and Bot-2 consistently
        # quote in the Round-1 data.
        #
        # PLACEHOLDER: uses a fixed value of 10_000.
        # Replace 10_000.0 with the actual fair value you calibrate.
        fv = np.full(n_steps, 10_000.0)
    elif product == "INTARIAN_PEPPER_ROOT":
        # ── USER: replace with calibrated latent process ─────────────────────
        # This is the TOMATOES analogue.  Candidate processes:
        #   - Gaussian random walk (kappa=0 OU)
        #   - Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (mean-reverting)
        #   - Regime-switching model
        #
        # Parameters to calibrate from Round-1 price CSV:
        #   mu    : long-run mean (read from data)
        #   kappa : mean-reversion speed (fit to ACF of price changes)
        #   sigma : per-tick diffusion (fit to std of price changes)
        #
        # PLACEHOLDER: raises an error so you don't accidentally run with
        # wrong parameters.
        raise NotImplementedError(`
            "\n"`
            "╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n"
            "║  USER MUST IMPLEMENT PRICE SIGNALS FOR INTARIAN_PEPPER_ROOT  ║\n"
            "║                                                              ║\n"
            "║  Edit: backtester/src/prosperity4mcbt/round1_products.py     ║\n"
            "║  Function: _round1_signals, branch: INTARIAN_PEPPER_ROOT     ║\n"
            "╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝"
        )
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown Round-1 product: {product}")
    ticks = np.arange(n_steps)
    mid = np.round(fv).astype(int)
    return pd.DataFrame({`
        "tick": ticks,`
        "fair_value": fv,`
        "mid_price": mid.astype(float),`
        "best_bid": mid - 1,`
        "best_ask": mid + 1,`
    })
EOF
cat > backtester/src/prosperity4mcbt/policy_optimizer.py << 'EOF'
"""`
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
EMPIRICAL POLICY OPTIMIZER`
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────`
`
Runs N Monte Carlo sessions, records the *empirically optimal* position size`
at every tick in hindsight, then fits a single closed-form function:`
`
    position_target = f(mispricing, time_left, vol, inventory, ...)`
`
The fitted function is output as clean Python code you can paste directly`
into your live Trader.`
`
Usage (via CLI)`
───────────────`
    prosperity4mcbt your_trader.py --round 1 --optimize-policy \`
        --sessions 3000 --opt-out fitted_policy.py`
`
Algorithm`
─────────`
1. Run N independent MC sessions, collecting TickSnapshots.`
2. For each tick, compute the *hindsight-optimal position*:`
       argmax_{pos ∈ [-lim, lim]} Σ_{t'=t}^{T} cash_flow(pos, fv_{t'})`
   Under a simple greedy model this reduces to:`
       sign(future_avg_return) * position_limit   if |future_return| > threshold`
       else 0`
   In practice we use the sign of the sum of future fair-value changes,`
   clipped to the position limit.`
3. Collect a giant DataFrame of (features, labels).`
4. Fit a parametric function family using scipy.optimize.curve_fit.`
5. Output the fitted function as Python source code.`
`
Fitting model`
─────────────`
Default model (the one iawa described as "a single simple mathematical
function"):`
`
    pos* = L * tanh(α * mispricing / vol) * tanh(β * time_left / T) * (1 - γ * |inv| / L)`
`
where:`
    L   = position limit (80)`
    T   = total ticks (10_000)`
    α   = aggressiveness of mispricing signal`
    β   = time-decay exponent (hold near end of day)`
    γ   = inventory-aversion factor`
`
This is a 3-parameter family that fits in ~seconds on a modern laptop.`
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import textwrap
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy.optimize import curve_fit
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Constants
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
POSITION_LIMIT = 80
TOTAL_TICKS = 10_000
_RUST_BIN = Path(__file__).parents[3] / "rust_simulator" / "target" / "release" / "rust_simulator"
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Hindsight-optimal label computation
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def compute_hindsight_labels(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """`
    Given a DataFrame of TickSnapshots (from one session), compute the`
    hindsight-optimal position at each tick.`
`
    Strategy: at tick t, the optimal position is:`
        sign(Σ_{t'=t}^{T} Δfv_{t'}) * position_limit`
`
    i.e. go max-long if fair value will (on average) go up, max-short if down,`
    and 0 if the future sum is negligible relative to the spread.`
`
    This is a greedy upper bound — it tells you what a perfect oracle would do.`
    The fitted function is a smooth approximation of this oracle.`
`
    Parameters`
    ----------`
    df : DataFrame with columns [tick, product, session_id, fair_value]`
`
    Returns`
    -------`
    df with added column 'optimal_position_hindsight'`
    """
    df = df.copy().sort_values(["session_id", "product", "tick"])
    def label_group(g: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        fv = g["fair_value"].values
        # Cumulative future return from tick t onwards: sum(fv[t+1:] - fv[t:-1])
        # = fv[-1] - fv[t]  (telescoping)
        future_return = fv[-1] - fv  # shape (n_ticks,)
        # Scale by half-spread to avoid over-trading on micro-fluctuations
        half_spread = g["spread_half"].values.mean()
        threshold = half_spread * 0.5
        pos = np.where(`
            future_return > threshold, POSITION_LIMIT,`
            np.where(future_return < -threshold, -POSITION_LIMIT, 0),`
        ).astype(int)
        g = g.copy()
        g["optimal_position_hindsight"] = pos
        return g
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# The parametric model to fit
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def sizing_model(`
    X: np.ndarray,`
    alpha: float,`
    beta: float,`
    gamma: float,`
) -> np.ndarray:
    """`
    Parametric position-sizing model.`
`
    pos* = L * tanh(α * mispricing / (vol + ε))`
               * tanh(β * time_left / T)`
               * (1 - γ * |inventory| / L)`
`
    Parameters`
    ----------`
    X      : (n_samples, 4) array of [mispricing, time_left, vol, inventory]`
    alpha  : mispricing sensitivity`
    beta   : time-left sensitivity`
    gamma  : inventory aversion`
`
    Returns`
    -------`
    pos : (n_samples,) float array in [-L, L]`
    """
    mispricing = X[:, 0]
    time_left  = X[:, 1]
    vol        = X[:, 2]
    inventory  = X[:, 3]
    eps = 1e-6  # avoid division by zero
    signal   = np.tanh(alpha * mispricing / (vol + eps))
    time_fac = np.tanh(beta  * time_left  / TOTAL_TICKS)
    inv_fac  = np.clip(1.0 - gamma * np.abs(inventory) / POSITION_LIMIT, 0.0, 1.0)
    return POSITION_LIMIT * signal * time_fac * inv_fac
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Main optimizer
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    rust_bin: Optional[str] = None,`
    seed_offset: int = 0,`
    sample_every: int = 10,  # record every Nth tick to keep memory manageable`

) -> dict:
    print(f"[policy_optimizer] Running {n_sessions} MC sessions for round {round_id}…")

            "Build with: cd rust_simulator && cargo build --release"
        )
    import tempfile

        snap_path = f.name
    cmd = [

$path = ".gitignore"
$content = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($path)
$entries = @(`
    "catboost_info/",`
    "experiments/BOB/",`
    "*.egg-info/"`
)
foreach ($entry in $entries) {`
    if ($content -notmatch [regex]::Escape($entry)) {`
        $content += "`r`n$entry"`
    }`
}
[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText(`
    $path,`
    $content,`
    [System.Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)`
)
